# Corporation Tax Act 2010

> **Warning: changes not yet applied to this text (16).** Text as at 2026-03-18. See https://www.legislation.gov.uk/changes/affected/ukpga/2010/4
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, Northern Ireland.

*Extent: E+W+S+N.I.*

Corporation Tax Act 2010

2010 c. 4

An Act to restate, with minor changes, certain enactments relating to corporation tax and certain enactments relating to company distributions; and for connected purposes.

\[3rd March 2010\]

Be it enacted by the Queen's most Excellent Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the Lords Spiritual and Temporal, and Commons, in this present Parliament assembled, and by the authority of the same, as follows:—[^key-98457113039e853d9f7c5d7b6fa5ac60][^key-09bf7fc4ae8df574c1dd6d007ad63e94][^key-769b2a6ff19c5b5a136b396714b12c2a]
